1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested a two-step task on server westd-38929:
   - **Step 1**: Kill zombie PID 95670 on GPU 2 (dead k=0.3 seed43 process occupying 15607MB). Only kill this specific PID per M040 rule - don't touch PIDs on GPU 0 (159683), GPU 1 (159765), or GPU 3 (159944).
   - **Step 2**: Set up and run a LIBERO-Plus smoke test on GPU 2 using the OpenVLA LIBERO-Object checkpoint. This is described as "最高价值单实验" (highest value single experiment) that gates the entire C7 experiment line. The goal is to verify LIBERO-Plus works as a robustness benchmark, testing OpenVLA on L1 camera perturbation, targeting SR > 30% as the viability threshold.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **LIBERO-Plus**: A drop-in replacement for LIBERO benchmark with 7 perturbation dimensions (Camera Viewpoints, Robot Initial States, Language Instructions, Light Conditions, Background Textures, Sensor Noise, Objects Layout), each with difficulty levels L1-L5
   - **Camera Viewpoint Perturbation**: Encoded in task names as `_view_{horizon}_{vertical}_{scale}_{rot}_{vert}_initstate_{n}`. The `OffScreenRenderEnv` parses these parameters and applies camera viewpoint changes internally
   - **LIBERO-Plus Benchmark API**: Uses same API as LIBERO (`benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[suite]()`) but with expanded task maps (2518 tasks for libero_object vs original 10)
   - **OpenVLA evaluation**: Model loaded from `/root/autodl-tmp/openvla-libero-object`, uses `UNNORM_KEY="libero_object"`, generates actions from image observations via prompt-based inference
   - **LIBERO-Plus evaluation differs from LIBERO**: Each task IS a specific perturbation, so `num_trials_per_task=1` (vs 50 in original LIBERO)
   - **PyTorch 2.6 breaking change**: `torch.load` defaults to `weights_only=True`, requiring explicit `weights_only=False` for LIBERO init_states files

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_openvla_liberoplus_smoke.py`** (CREATED)
     - The main smoke test script, based on existing `eval_openvla_libero.py`
     - Key additions: task filtering by category/difficulty, clean language extraction from BDDL files, init_states loading with `weights_only=False`, `_view_` path handling
     - Full final script (key functions):
     ```python
     def get_filtered_task_indices(suite_name, category, difficulty):
         with open(TASK_CLASSIFICATION) as f:
             tc = json.load(f)
         filtered = []
         for t in tc[suite_name]:
             if t.get("category") == category and t.get("difficulty_level") == difficulty:
                 filtered.append(t["id"] - 1)  # 1-indexed to 0-indexed
         return filtered

     def get_clean_language(task_name, bddl_files_dir, problem_folder):
         import libero.libero.envs.bddl_utils as BDDLUtils
         base_name = task_name
         if "_view_" in base_name:
             base_name = base_name.split("_view_")[0]
         if "_noise_" in base_name:
             base_name = base_name.split("_noise_")[0]
         bddl_path = os.path.join(bddl_files_dir, problem_folder, base_name + ".bddl")
         if os.path.exists(bddl_path):
             try:
                 info = BDDLUtils.get_problem_info(bddl_path)
                 return info["language_instruction"]
             except Exception:
                 pass
         return " ".join(base_name.split("_"))

     def load_init_states(task, init_states_dir):
         init_file = task.init_states_file
         if "_view_" in init_file:
             base = init_file.split("_view_")[0]
             ext = init_file.split(".")[-1]
             init_file = f"{base}.{ext}"
         # ... similar handling for _table_, _tb_, _light_, _add_, _level
         path = os.path.join(init_states_dir, task.problem_folder, init_file)
         init_states = torch.load(path, weights_only=False)
         return init_states
     ```

   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_openvla_libero.py`** (READ, 288 lines)

       if "_view_" in bddl_file_name and "_initstate_" in bddl_file_name:

           angle_view, init_state = angle_view_initstate.split("_initstate_")

### Assistant

_source line 405_

## LIBERO-Plus Smoke Test 结果

| 指标 | 值 |
|------|-----|
| **Overall SR** | **3.3% (1/30)** |
| 耗时 | 22.0 min |
| Task 1 (view_0_0_100_2_4) | 0/10 (0%) |
| Task 2 (view_0_0_100_2_8) | 1/10 (10%) |
| Task 3 (view_0_0_100_2_350) | 0/10 (0%) |

**结论：SR = 3.3%，远低于 30% 阈值。**

这与 LIBERO-Plus 排行榜上 OpenVLA Camera SR = 0.8% 一致（我们 3 task 采样方差导致略高）。即使是最轻微的 L1 Camera Viewpoint 扰动，vanilla OpenVLA 也几乎完全失败。

**对 C7 实验线的含义：**
- LIBERO-Plus 作为 robustness benchmark **可用** — 它确实能有效暴露 VLA 对 camera perturbation 的脆弱性
- 但 vanilla OpenVLA baseline SR 太低（~3%），直接用作压缩前后对比的 baseline 意义不大（地板效应）
- 如果 C7 目标是证明 "压缩后鲁棒性不降"，需要先找到一个在 LIBERO-Plus 上有合理 baseline SR 的模型/方法，或者换一个更温和的扰动维度/难度
